session_results: list of (mouse_id, session_data, trials)\n    session_results = []\n    subjects = []  # ordered unique mouse IDs\n    mouse_to_subj_idx = {}\n\n    global_session_count = 0\n    for mouse_id in all_mouse_ids:\n        mouse_exps = vb_experiments[vb_experiments.mouse_id == mouse_id]\n        mouse_sessions = mouse_exps.drop_duplicates(subset='ophys_session_id')[\n            ['ophys_session_id', 'session_type', 'date_of_acquisition']\n        ].sort_values(by='date_of_acquisition')\n\n        session_ids = mouse_sessions['ophys_session_id'].values\n        if args.sample:\n            session_ids = session_ids[:2]\n\n        # Register subject\n        if mouse_id not in mouse_to_subj_idx:\n            mouse_to_subj_idx[mouse_id] = len(subjects)\n            subjects.append(str(mouse_id))\n\n        print(f\"\\nMouse {mouse_id} ({mouse_exps.iloc[0]['cre_line']}): \"\n              f\"{len(session_ids)} sessions\")\n\n        for j, sid in enumerate(session_ids):\n            stype = mouse_sessions[mouse_sessions.ophys_session_id == sid].iloc[0]['session_type']\n            sess_exps = mouse_exps[mouse_exps.ophys_session_id == sid]\n            global_session_count += 1\n            print(f'  [{j+1}/{len(session_ids)}] Session {sid} ({stype}) '\n                  f'— {len(sess_exps)} planes...', end=' ', flush=True)\n\n            t1 = time.time()\n            try:\n                session_data = extract_session_data(bc, sess_exps)\n                trials = segment_trials(session_data)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f'FAILED: {e}')\n                continue\n            elapsed = time.time() - t1\n            print(f'{session_data[\"neural_data\"].shape[0]} neurons, '\n                  f'{len(trials)} trials ({elapsed:.1f}s)')\n\n            # Keep only small metadata; drop large full-session arrays\n            session_meta = {\n                'ophys_ts': session_data['ophys_ts'],\n                'plane_labels': session_data['plane_labels'],\n            }\n            del session_data\n            session_results.append((mouse_id, session_meta, trials))\n\n    print(f\"\\nExtracted {len(session_results)} sessions from \"\n          f\"{len(subjects)} mice\")\n\n    # ---- Compute discretization bin edges from ALL trials ----\n    print(\"\\n=== Computing discretization bin edges ===\")\n    all_running = np.concatenate([\n        np.concatenate([t['running'] for t in trials])\n        for _, _, trials in session_results if len(trials) > 0\n    ])\n    all_pupil = np.concatenate([\n        np.concatenate([t['pupil'] for t in trials])\n        for _, _, trials in session_results if len(trials) > 0\n    ])\n\n    run_edges = discretize(all_running, N_LEVELS)\n    pupil_edges = discretize(all_pupil, N_LEVELS)\n    print(f\"Running speed bin edges: {run_edges}\")\n    print(f\"Pupil diameter bin edges: {pupil_edges}\")\n\n    # ---- Build image code mapping (global across all sessions) ----\n    all_image_names = set()\n    for _, _, trials in session_results:\n        for t in trials:\n            all_image_names.update(t['image_names'])\n    all_image_names = sorted(all_image_names)\n    image_to_code = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(all_image_names)}\n    print(f\"\\nImage codes ({len(all_image_names)} images): {image_to_code}\")\n\n    # Trial outcome mapping\n    outcome_to_code = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(TRIAL_OUTCOMES)}\n    print(f\"Trial outcome codes: {outcome_to_code}\")\n\n    # ---- Assemble output format ----\n    print(\"\\n=== Assembling output ===\")\n    all_neural = []\n\"\"\"Convert Allen Institute Visual Behavior 2P data into decoder-ready format.\n\nDesign decisions\n----------------\n* Task: the change-detection (Visual Behavior) task; only active behavior sessions\n  are used (in passive sessions the lick spout is retracted, so there are no\n  choices, rewards or trial outcomes to decode).\n* Experience level: familiar image set only, following the reference paper, which\n  restricted its neural analysis to familiar stimuli.  Every familiar active session\n  in this release used image set A, so the eight image identities are the same in\n  every session and share one set of output labels.\n* Sessions: one converted session per ophys session.  For Multiscope (multi-plane)\n  sessions the simultaneously imaged planes are merged into a single session, with\n  each neuron labeled by the brain region of its plane (VISp / VISl).\n* Trials: rows of the SDK trials table with go == True or catch == True.  This\n  excludes the aborted and auto-rewarded trials by construction.\n* Temporal alignment: trials are aligned to the change time (the sham change time on\n  catch trials) and binned on a fixed 250 ms grid (the duration of one image flash)\n  with a bin edge exactly at the change time, so bin edges are locked to the\n  stimulus flash cycle.  The grid is shared by all data streams; each stream is\n  binned from its own native timestamps (ophys frame times for the neural data,\n  stimulus times for the images, behavior/eye camera times for running and pupil).\n  A fixed bin also makes single-plane (~31 Hz) and Multiscope (~11 Hz) recordings\n  commensurate.\n* Neural data: detected calcium events (discrete events regressed from the df/f\n  traces by the AllenSDK event detection), as used in the reference paper, summed\n  within each time bin.\n* Stimulus variables follow the reference paper's convention of assigning data to\n  the 750 ms image presentation interval (250 ms flash + 500 ms gray screen), so the\n  image identity is held across the gray screen and across omitted flashes.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport glob\nimport os\nimport pickle\nimport warnings\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\nCACHE_DIR = '/app/data'\nBIN_SIZE = 0.25          # s, duration of one image flash\nFLASH_INTERVAL = 0.75    # s, image presentation interval (250 ms image + 500 ms gray)\nNQUANTILES = 5\nOUTCOME_NAMES = ['hit', 'miss', 'false_alarm', 'correct_reject']\n\n\ndef get_experiment_table():\n    tbl = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(\n        CACHE_DIR, 'visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0', 'project_metadata',\n        'ophys_experiment_table.csv'))\n    have = [int(os.path.basename(f).split('_')[-1].split('.')[0]) for f in glob.glob(\n        os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, 'visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0',\n                     'behavior_ophys_experiments', '*.nwb'))]\n    return tbl[tbl.ophys_experiment_id.isin(have)]\n\n\ndef select_sessions():\n    \"\"\"Return {ophys_session_id: [ophys_experiment_id, ...]} for the sessions used.\"\"\"\n    tbl = get_experiment_table()\n    sel = tbl[(tbl.behavior_type == 'active_behavior') &      # mouse doing the task\n              (tbl.experience_level == 'Familiar')]           # familiar image set\n    sel = sel.sort_values(['ophys_session_id', 'ophys_experiment_id'])\n    return sel.groupby('ophys_session_id').ophys_experiment_id.apply(list).to_dict()\n\n\ndef interp_nans(x):\n    \"\"\"Linearly interpolate NaNs (blinks / lost tracking) in a 1d time series.\"\"\"\n    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    bad = ~np.isfinite(x)\n    if bad.all():\n        return None\n    if bad.any():\n        idx = np.arange(len(x))\n        x = x.copy()\n        x[bad] = np.interp(idx[bad], idx[~bad], x[~bad])\n    return x\n\n\ndef quantile_bins(values, nbins=NQUANTILES):\n    \"\"\"Discretize into nbins equal-percentile bins; returns int codes 0..nbins-1.\"\"\"\n    edges = np.quantile(values, np.linspace(0, 1, nbins + 1)[1:-1])\n    return np.searchsorted(edges, values, side='right').astype(np.int64)\n\n\ndef bin_sum(values_cumsum, timestamps, edges):\n    \"\"\"Sum of values within each bin of `edges`, plus the sample count per bin.\"\"\"\n    idx = np.searchsorted(timestamps, edges)\n    if values_cumsum.ndim == 1:\n        return values_cumsum[idx[1:]] - values_cumsum[idx[:-1]], np.diff(idx)\n    return (values_cumsum[:, idx[1:]] - values_cumsum[:, idx[:-1]]), np.diff(idx)\n\n\ndef convert_session(osid, oeids, neural_key='events'):\n    from allensdk.brain_observatory.behavior.behavior_project_cache import (\n        VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache)\n    cache = VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache.from_local_cache(cache_dir=CACHE_DIR)\n\n    planes = []\n    for oeid in oeids:\n        ds = cache.get_behavior_ophys_experiment(int(oeid))\n        ts = np.asarray(ds.ophys_timestamps)\n        ev = ds.events\n        if len(ev) == 0:\n            continue\n        act = np.vstack(ev[neural_key].values).astype(np.float64)\n        assert act.shape[1] == len(ts)\n        planes.append({\n            'oeid': int(oeid), 'ds': ds, 'ts': ts,\n            'cum': np.concatenate([np.zeros((act.shape[0], 1)),\n                                   np.cumsum(act, axis=1)], axis=1),\n            'ncells': act.shape[0],\n            'cell_ids': [int(c) for c in ev.index.values],\n            'region': str(ds.metadata['targeted_structure']),\n            'depth': int(ds.metadata['imaging_depth']),\n            'frame_rate': float(1.0 / np.median(np.diff(ts))),\n        })\n    if not planes:\n        return None\n\n    ds = planes[0]['ds']     # behavior streams are shared by all planes of a session\n\n    # ---- behavior: running speed and pupil diameter on their native clocks ----\n    run = ds.running_speed\n    run_t = run.timestamps.values\n    run_v = interp_nans(run.speed.values)\n    if run_v is None:\n        return None\n    run_cum = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(run_v)])\n\n    try:\n        eye = ds.eye_tracking\n    except Exception:\n        eye = None\n    if eye is None or len(eye) == 0:\n        return None              # no eye tracking -> pupil output cannot be made\n    pupil_area = interp_nans(eye.pupil_area.values)     # NaN on likely-blink frames\n    if pupil_area is None:\n        return None\n    pupil_v = 2.0 * np.sqrt(np.maximum(pupil_area, 0.0) / np.pi)   # diameter, pixels\n    eye_t = eye.timestamps.values\n    pupil_cum = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(pupil_v)])\n\n    # ---- stimulus presentations of the change detection block ----\n    sp = ds.stimulus_presentations\n    sp = sp[sp.stimulus_block_name == 'change_detection_behavior']\n    image_names = sorted(set(sp.image_name.unique()) - {'omitted'})\n    shown = sp[(~sp.omitted) & (sp.image_name != 'omitted')].sort_values('start_time')\n    pres_start = shown.start_time.values\n    pres_img = np.array([image_names.index(n) for n in shown.image_name.values])\n    change_start = pres_start[shown.is_change.values]\n\n    # ---- trials: go and catch only (excludes aborted and auto-rewarded) ----\n    tr = ds.trials\n    tr = tr[(tr.go | tr.catch) & tr.change_time.notna()]\n\n    tmin = max([p['ts'][0] for p in planes] + [run_t[0], eye_t[0]])\n    tmax = min([p['ts'][-1] for p in planes] + [run_t[-1], eye_t[-1]])\n\n    trials = []\n    for _, trial in tr.iterrows():\n        oc = [k for k in OUTCOME_NAMES if bool(trial[k])]\n        if len(oc) != 1:\n            continue\n        # fixed 250 ms bins with an edge exactly at the (sham) change time\n        k0 = int(np.ceil((trial.start_time - trial.change_time) / BIN_SIZE))\n        k1 = int(np.floor((trial.stop_time - trial.change_time) / BIN_SIZE))\n        edges = trial.change_time + BIN_SIZE * np.arange(k0, k1 + 1)\n        if len(edges) < 3 or edges[0] < tmin or edges[-1] > tmax:\n            continue             # trial not fully covered by all data streams\n        centers = 0.5 * (edges[:-1] + edges[1:])\n\n        # neural: calcium events summed within each bin, planes stacked\n        neural_parts, ok = [], True\n        for p in planes:\n            vals, nfr = bin_sum(p['cum'], p['ts'], edges)\n            if np.any(nfr < 1):\n                ok = False\n                break\n            neural_parts.append(vals)\n        if not ok:\n            continue\n        neural_trial = np.vstack(neural_parts).astype(np.float32)\n\n        # behavior: mean within each bin\n        rsum, rn = bin_sum(run_cum, run_t, edges)\n        psum, pn = bin_sum(pupil_cum, eye_t, edges)\n        if np.any(rn < 1) or np.any(pn < 1):\n            continue\n        run_trial = rsum / rn\n        pupil_trial = psum / pn\n\n        # stimulus: identity of the most recent image flash, held over the 750 ms\n        # image presentation interval and over omitted flashes\n        j = np.searchsorted(pres_start, centers, side='right') - 1\n        if np.any(j < 0):\n            continue\n        image_trial = pres_img[j].astype(np.int64)\n        # image change: 1 during the presentation interval of the changed image\n        jc = np.searchsorted(change_start, centers, side='right') - 1\n        change_trial = np.zeros(len(centers), dtype=np.int64)\n        good = jc >= 0\n        change_trial[good] = (\n            (centers[good] - change_start[jc[good]]) < FLASH_INTERVAL).astype(np.int64)\n\n        trials.append({'neural': neural_trial, 'run': run_trial, 'pupil': pupil_trial,\n                       'image': image_trial, 'change': change_trial,\n                       'outcome': OUTCOME_NAMES.index(oc[0])})\n\n    if len(trials) < 2:\n        return None\n\n    # Running speed and pupil diameter are discretized into five equal-percentile\n    # bins computed within each session, over the time bins included in the trials.\n    # Mice differ strongly in }\n    return {'neural': neural, 'output': output, 'info': info,\n            'regions': regions, 'image_names': image_names}\n\n\ndef _worker(args):\n    osid, oeids, neural_key = args\n    try:\n --- Neural: merge dF/F across planes ---\n    dff_list = []\n    plane_labels = []\n    for exp_id, ds in datasets.items():\n        dff_list.append(np.vstack(ds.dff_traces.dff.values))\n        area = session_experiments.loc[exp_id, 'targeted_structure']\n        depth = session_experiments.loc[exp_id, 'imaging_depth']\n        plane_labels.extend([f'{area}_{depth}um'] * len(ds.dff_traces))\n    neural_data = np.vstack(dff_list)  # NaN -> 0 (safest discrete label).\"\"\"\n    out = np.digitize(values, bin_edges[1:-1]).astype(np.int8)\n    out[np.isnan(values)] = 0\n    return out\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description='Convert Allen Visual Behavior Ophys data to pickle format')\n    parser.add_argument('output', help='Output pickle file path')\n    parser.add_argument('--datadir', type=str,\n                        default='/groups/zhang/home/zhangl5/Allen_Brain',\n                        help='Allen SDK cache directory (default: /groups/zhang/home/zhangl5/Allen_Brain)')\n    mode = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\n    mode.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True,\n                      help='Process all mice and sessions (default)')\n    mode.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true',\n                      help='Process only 2 mice with 2 sessions each for testing')\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    print(\"Loading Allen cache...\")\n    bc = get_cache(args.datadir)\n    experiment_table = bc.get_ophys_experiment_table()\n\n    # Filter to VisualBehavior project only\n    vb_experiments = experiment_table[experiment_table.project_code == PROJECT_CODE]\n\n    # The datalimit dataset holds a subset of the release, listed in\n    # DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv. First, statistics of the converted data and decoder accuracy will be compared to those of an expert-written solution. Second, each decision you make about how to load, filter, process, align, and save the data will be compared with the decision made by an expert. A decision that differs from the expert's is acceptable if it is equally well justified; decisions that are poorly justified will be flagged as concerning or incorrect.\n\n## Reference Information\n\n- Reference **papers**:\n  - The whitepaper about the dataset, \"Allen Brain Observatory: Visual Behavior 2P Technical Whitepaper\", is in the file `/app/whitepaper.pdf`.\n  - A paper using this dataset, \"Behavioral strategy shapes activation of the Vip-Sst disinhibitory circuit in visual cortex\", is in the file `/app/paper.pdf`.\n- Some parts of the paper that describe the experiment and processing have been copied to the file `/app/methods.txt`. \n- **Code**:\n  - The Allen data **SDK** is in the directory `/app/code`.\n  - Tutorials about using the data are in the directory `/app/tutorials`.\n- **Data** from the paper are in the directory `/app/data`.\n\n## Decoder Task\n\n- Collect and convert data under the \"Visual Behavior\" task.\n- Temporally align based on ophys timestamp.\n- Segment each recording session into individual trials based on how they are defined in the experiment. Include both the \"Go\" and \"Catch\" trials, but exclude the \"Aborted\" and \"Auto-rewarded\" trials.\n\n### Decoder Inputs:\n\nNo inputs for this task.\n\n### Decoder Outputs\n\n- Image identity (of the image presented during the non-grey screen), categorical variable. Time-varying.\n- Image change, binary variable. Have value of 1 right after a change in image identity, otherwise 0. Time-varying.\n- Running speed, discretized into five equal percentile bins. Time-varying.\n- Pupil diameter, discretized into five equal percentile bins.  Time-varying.\n- Trial outcome. Static per-trial.\n\n## Target Data Format\n\n- Save the full converted dataset to the pickle file `/app/converted_data.pkl`.\n- All datasets must be converted into the following Python dictionary structure:\n\n```python\ndata = {\n    'neural': [  # list of sessions\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\n            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # shape: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # trial 2\n            ...\n        ],\n        [  # list of trials for session 2\n            ...\n        ],\n        ...\n    ],\n\n    'input': [  # list of sessions\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\n            input_data,  # stimulus/task variables, shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input)\n            ...\n        ],\n        ...\n    ],\n\n    'output': [  # list of sessions\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\n            output_data,  # behavioral response, shape (n_output, n_timepoints) or (n_output)\n            ...\n        ],\n        ...\n    ],\n\n    'subjects': list of str, # ids of all subjects, length n_subjects\n    'subject_idx': shape (n_sessions,), # index into subjects for each session\n\n    'brain_regions': list of str, # names of the brain regions recorded from, e.g. ALM, V1\n    'brain_region_idx': [ # list of sessions\n        neuron_region_idx, # shape: (n_neurons,)\n    ], # index into `brain_regions` for each neuron\n\n    'input_names': list of str, # names for each input, of length n_input\n    'output_names': list of str, # names of each output, of length n_output\n    'output_values': [ # list of length n_output\n        [ output_val0, ... ], # list of str of length d_output[0], name of each output value\n        ...\n    ],\n\n    'metadata': {\n        'task_description': str, # description of output tasks\n        'time_bin_size': float, # length of time bin in ms\n        'temporal_alignment_event': str, # description of alignment event\n        'off_start': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to start of trial in seconds, None if N/A\n        'off_end': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to end of trial in seconds, None if N/A\n        ...\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Key Format Requirements\n\n- **neural**: Neural activity data (e.g., firing rates, spike counts) organized by session and trial\n  - Dimensions: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for each trial.\n  - Time bins should be the same size for all trials and sessions. \n  - There needs to be at least two trials within each session in order to evaluate the decoder performance.\n\n- **input**: Variables that serve as inputs **to the decoder**\n  - These are **decoder inputs**. They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\n  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\n    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\n    - audio: frequency, amplitude\n    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\n  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \n  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\n  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\n  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\n  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \n  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\n  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\n  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\n- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\n  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\n\n- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\n- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\n\n- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\n  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \n\n- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\n  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\n- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\n  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\n\n- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \n  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\n  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\n  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \n  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n\n## Decoder Reference\n\n- Run this script with `python /app/train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n- Optional arguments:\n  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n\n### Purpose\n\nThese functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n1. Data structure is correct - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n2. Dimensions are consistent - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n3. Values are sensible - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n4. Decoder can successfully train and predict\n5. Poor performance indicates data formatting issues that need investigation\n\n### Consistency requirements\n\nYour processing and formatting must **match the reference papers and code** with respect to:\n- Loading of data\n- Temporal alignment of different time series streams (neural, inputs, outputs)\n- Processing of neural, input, and output data streams\n- Curation of data: filtering of low-quality neurons, trials, sessions, and mice.\n\nDiscrepancies are only allowed if required by the Decoder Input and Decoder Output specifications above, or the task of training a neural decoder. During testing, a reference copy of `/app/train_decoder.py` will be run on your converted data. Data statistics and decoder accuracy will be compared to those achieved by human-written conversion code.\n\n## Success Criteria\n\nA successful conversion should:\n1. Two refinements to my draft: (1) segment trials using the trials-table start_time/stop_time (the experiment's own trial definition), giving variable-length trials aligned on ophys frames; (2) assign image identity and change to the 750 ms image-presentation interval (flash + following gray), following the paper's convention of assigning events to image presentation intervals, so identity carries through gray screens and omissions.\",\n  \"plan\": \"Rewrite convert_data.py accordingly, then run a pilot on ~6 experiments to validate the format and decoder.\",\n  \"commands\": [\n    {\"keystrokes\": \"cat > /app/convert_data.py << 'PYEOF'\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Convert Allen Institute Visual Behavior 2P data into decoder-ready format.\\n\\nDesign decisions\\n----------------\\n* Task: the change-detection (\\\"Visual Behavior\\\") task, active behavior sessions only\\n  (passive sessions have no licking / trial outcomes).\\n* Experience level: Familiar image set only, following the reference paper which\\n  restricted the neural analysis to familiar stimuli.  In this data release every\\n  familiar active session used image set A, so the eight image identities are the\\n  same in every session and can share one set of output labels.\\n* Rig: single-plane experiments (project_code == 'VisualBehavior').  These are all\\n  sampled at ~30.94 Hz so the native ophys frame period can be used as a common time\\n  bin for every session; Multiscope planes are sampled at ~11 Hz on plane-specific\\n  grids and would break the constant-bin requirement.\\n* Trials: rows of the SDK trials table with go == True or catch == True, taken over\\n  their full extent [start_time, stop_time).  This excludes aborted and\\n  auto-rewarded trials by construction.\\n* Temporal alignment: the ophys (2-photon) frame timestamps are the common clock.\\n  Running speed, pupil diameter and the stimulus time series are resampled onto\\n  those timestamps.\\n* Neural data: detected calcium events (the event-detection output that regresses\\n  discrete events out of the df/f traces), as used in the reference paper.\\n* Stimulus variables are assigned to the 750 ms image-presentation interval (250 ms\\n  flash + 500 ms gray) as in the reference paper, so the image identity is carried\\n  through the gray screen and through omitted flashes.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport glob\\nimport os\\nimport pickle\\nimport warnings\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport pandas as pd\\n\\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\\n\\nCACHE_DIR = '/app/data'\\nNQUANTILES = 5\\nFLASH_INTERVAL = 0.75   # s, image presentation interval (250 ms image + 500 ms gray)\\nOUTCOME_NAMES = ['hit', 'miss', 'false_alarm', 'correct_reject']\\n\\n\\ndef get_experiment_table():\\n    tbl = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(\\n        CACHE_DIR, 'visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0', 'project_metadata',\\n        'ophys_experiment_table.csv'))\\n    have = [int(os.path.basename(f).split('_')[-1].split('.')[0]) for f in glob.glob(\\n        os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, 'visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0',\\n                     'behavior_ophys_experiments', '*.nwb'))]\\n    return tbl[tbl.ophys_experiment_id.isin(have)]\\n\\n\\ndef select_experiments():\\n    tbl = get_experiment_table()\\n    sel = tbl[(tbl.behavior_type == 'active_behavior') &     # mouse performing the task\\n              (tbl.experience_level == 'Familiar') &         # familiar image set\\n              (tbl.project_code == 'VisualBehavior')]        # single-plane rig\\n    return sel.sort_values('ophys_experiment_id').reset_index(drop=True)\\n\\n\\ndef interp_nans(x):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Linearly interpolate NaNs (blinks / lost tracking) in a 1d time series.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\\n    bad = ~np.isfinite(x)\\n    if bad.all():\\n        return None\\n    if bad.any():\\n        idx = np.arange(len(x))\\n        x = x.copy()\\n        x[bad] = np.interp(idx[bad], idx[~bad], x[~bad])\\n    return x\\n\\n\\ndef quantile_bins(values, nbins=NQUANTILES):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Discretize into nbins equal-percentile bins; returns int codes 0..nbins-1.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    edges = np.quantile(values, np.linspace(0, 1, nbins + 1)[1:-1])\\n    return np.searchsorted(edges, values, side='right').astype(np.int64)\\n\\n\\ndef convert_experiment(oeid, neural_key='events'):\\n    from allensdk.brain_observatory.behavior.behavior_project_cache import (\\n        VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache)\\n    cache = VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache.from_local_cache(cache_dir=CACHE_DIR)\\n    ds = cache.get_behavior_ophys_experiment(int(oeid))\\n\\n    ts = np.asarray(ds.ophys_timestamps)\\n    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(ts)))\\n    assert 0.03 < dt < 0.035, f'unexpected ophys frame period for {oeid}: {dt}'\\n\\n    # ---- neural activity: detected calcium events, (ncells, nframes) ----\\n    ev = ds.events\\n    neural_all = np.vstack(ev[neural_key].values).astype(np.float32)\\n    cell_ids = [int(c) for c in ev.index.values]\\n    assert neural_all.shape[1] == len(ts)\\n\\n    # ---- behavioral time series resampled onto the ophys timestamps ----\\n    run = ds.running_speed\\n    running = np.interp(ts, run.timestamps.values, run.speed.values)\\n\\n    try:\\n        eye = ds.eye_tracking\\n    except Exception:\\n        eye = None\\n    if eye is None or len(eye) == 0:\\n        return None            # no eye tracking -> pupil output cannot be produced\\n    pupil_area = interp_nans(eye.pupil_area.values)   # NaN on likely-blink frames\\n    if pupil_area is None:\\n        return None\\n    pupil_diam = 2.0 * np.sqrt(np.maximum(pupil_area, 0.0) / np.pi)\\n    pupil = np.interp(ts, eye.timestamps.values, pupil_diam)\\n\\n    # ---- stimulus time series on the ophys timestamps ----\\n    sp = ds.stimulus_presentations\\n    sp = sp[sp.stimulus_block_name == 'change_detection_behavior']\\n    image_names = sorted(set(sp.image_name.unique()) - {'omitted'})\\n    shown = sp[(~sp.omitted) & (sp.image_name != 'omitted')].sort_values('start_time')\\n    pres_start = shown.start_time.values\\n    pres_img = np.array([image_names.index(n) for n in shown.image_name.values])\\n    # each ophys frame is labeled with the most recent (non-omitted) image flash, i.e.\\n    # the identity is held over the 750 ms image presentation interval and over omissions\\n    j = np.searchsorted(pres_start, ts, side='right') - 1\\n    image_id = np.where(j >= 0, pres_img[np.maximum(j, 0)], -1).astype(np.int64)\\n    # image change: 1 over the presentation interval of the changed image\\n    change = np.zeros(len(ts), dtype=np.int64)\\n    for t0 in shown.start_time.values[shown.is_change.values]:\\n        i0, i1 = np.searchsorted(ts, [t0, t0 + FLASH_INTERVAL])\\n        change[i0:i1] = 1\\n\\n    # ---- trials: go and catch only (excludes aborted and auto-rewarded) ----\\n    tr = ds.trials\\n    tr = tr[(tr.go | tr.catch)]\\n\\n    windows, outcomes = [], []\\n    for _, trial in tr.iterrows():\\n        i0, i1 = np.searchsorted(ts, [trial.start_time, trial.stop_time])\\n        if i1 - i0 < 2 or i0 < 0 or i1 > len(ts):\\n            continue\\n        if np.any(image_id[i0:i1] < 0):\\n            continue           # no image shown yet (should not happen)\\n        oc = [k for k in OUTCOME_NAMES if bool(trial[k])]\\n        if len(oc) != 1:\\n            continue\\n        windows.append((int(i0), int(i1)))\\n        outcomes.append(OUTCOME_NAMES.index(oc[0]))\\n\\n    if len(windows) < 2:\\n        return None\\n\\n    # Running speed and pupil diameter are discretized into five equal-percentile bins\\n    # computed within each session, over the samples that are actually included in\\n    # trials.
